C4 also covers: the shape of `ownerPolicy` (§6.5.2) and of `additionalControlAuthorizations` (an array of 1..63 `{keyId, signature}` with §3.3 scalars), and `additionalControlAuthorizations` inside a migrated prefix, coded against `migratedFrom` → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-91.5] [A-95.2]; and the whole-string lowercase grammar of every PublicKey32 slot — an uppercase or mixed-case `credentialPublicKey`, owner key, attester or counterparty key in a native event or after a migrated prefix → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` at C4, before control authorization and before the §3.3 point test, with no normalization [A-157]. Every "(growth)"/"(not growth)" parenthetical below is informative; §5.2.1's formula decides [A-72.3]. C5 `DEPLOYMENT_INITIALIZED` after position 0 → `GENESIS_DUPLICATE`; duplicate `id` → `EVENT_ID_DUPLICATE`; `timestamp` < previous → `EVENT_TIME_REGRESSION`. Then, for every ◇ and ▲ type, the time rules T1 (under `ATTESTED`) and T3 (under a genesis `sessionTimeHorizonMs`) of §6.5.6 → `TRANSITION_INVALID`, before the type's own list and before its fence [A-172.1]. Then the type-specific steps below. **Last step, every type after position 0:** capacity (§5.2.1): step 1 `CAPACITY_EXCEEDED` (all types); step 2 `CAPACITY_RESERVED` (non-control); step 3 `CAPACITY_EXCEEDED` (non-control, `maxStateRecords`); step 4 `CAPACITY_CONTROL_ONLY` (non-control growth at `maxEvents`).

**A.3 Session authorization block "S(code)"** (§6.2): session exists and `credentialKeyId` matches → *code*; epoch fence → `SESSION_FENCED`; agent active and binding epoch equals the session's → *code*; not expired (`timestamp < expiresAt`; a null-expiry migrated session is expired outside the prefix) → *code*; `hash` equals recomputed challenge hash → *code*; signature verifies under the §6.3 profile → *code*. For admissions *code* = `TRANSITION_INVALID` (rc.1 reasons); for the outer ▲ session the fence is `SESSION_FENCED` and the rest is HF1 §8.4's code (`TRANSITION_INVALID`).

**A.4 The ◆ rows under an owner policy.** [A-115.1] [A-125.1] [A-170.4] Every ◆ row other than 0 reads, in this order: **step 0** (`M_eff(e)`, `PR_eff(e, σ(p))`, `isCheckpoint(e)` and, for row 26, `isRestoration(e)`, `isRecovery(e)` and `adm(e)`, decided from `data`, the `keyId`s of `S(e)`, the epoch record and `σ(p)` before any signature is verified, §6.5.3; Appendix F invariant 36); **control authorization** (§6.1; under a policy the §6.5.3 replay rule with `adm(e)` of §6.5.4 and the counting rule) → `CONTROL_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`; **T2** (§6.5.6, under `ATTESTED`) → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-172.1]; then the type's own checks as the row lists them. Without a policy, step 0 is trivial (`M_eff = 1`, `PR_eff` never consulted) and T2 is vacuous, so the rows read exactly as r4 [A-115.1] [A-125.1]. The rows below write "step 0; control authorization; T2" for this prefix.

| # | type | after the common prefix, in order |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | `DEPLOYMENT_INITIALIZED` | genesis schema (§5.1: `domain.version` → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID`; `versions` → `UNSUPPORTED_VERSION`; `sessionTimeHorizonMs` outside `U53 ≥ 1 \| null`, including `0` → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` at C4 [A-179.1 (A-22.6)]); each `ed25519` owner key per the §3.3 declaration test → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-32.2]; `ownerPolicy` validity when present (§6.5.2: rules 1–11, with rule 5's distinct credentials, rule 10 and rule 11 over the seven `PR_types` names and `LOW_types`, class by credential; the attestation rules 0–5 with the attester-credential rule — an `attesters` credential equal to a credential of `K`, in `foreign(σ(0))` (empty), or whose digest is in `D` — and `|attesters| ≥ 2` under `ATTESTED`; `historyId` absent at position 0; the policy-time rules (a)–(c); and, in this list, the commitment rule — no digest of `nextKeyDigests` equals the digest of a credential in `attCreds(P')` — since at the genesis every digest is introduced; forbidden with `migratedFrom`, rule 9) → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-95.2] [A-105.1] [A-115.1] [A-125.1] [A-173.2]; in a migrated genesis, `migratedFrom.sourceAttestation` (non-null) verified under `migratedFrom.genesis.ownerKeys` → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-40.2]; no capacity step |
| 1–7, 9, 11, 20, 25, 27, 28 | ◆ identity, roles, epochs, grants/revocations, anchors, witnesses | step 0; control authorization (§6.1, quorum per §6.5.3) → `CONTROL_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID` (in a migrated prefix also `sourceControlAuthorization` in the shadow context, `{position}`; 27–28 inside a prefix are `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` at C4 [A-78.1]); T2 → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-125.1]; rc.1-final transition preconditions (HF1 §6, §7.1–7.2) → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity step 1 only [A-170.4] |
| 8 | `RUNTIME_SESSION_ADMITTED` ◆ | step 0; control authorization (quorum per §6.5.3); T2 [A-125.1]; `credentialPublicKey` per the §3.3 declaration test → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-32.2]; rc.1 preconditions (`TRANSITION_INVALID`); committed, owner or attester credential under a policy (§6.5.7: while `P_w(p)` is present, a credential in `creds(K(p))`, whose digest is in `D(p)`, or in `attCreds(P(p))`) → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-110.2] [A-117.4] [A-125.1]; capacity step 1 |

| 12 | `TRANSACTION_INTENT_DECLARED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`DECLARATION_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); actor binding (session agent = `actorId`) → `DECLARATION_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`; nonce record exists for `(actorId, nonce)` and other rc.1 declaration preconditions → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (growth) |
| 13 | `TRANSACTION_INTENT_ADMITTED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); epoch fence → `SESSION_FENCED`; rc.1-final admission rules (session, binding, proof byte-equality, `evaluationTime == timestamp`, nonce record names the intent) → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; metric rule → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; budget procedure (any `BUDGET_*`/`AMOUNT_OVERFLOW` DENY) → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `usage` overflow → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–3 (lifecycle slot, not growth) |
| 14 | `TRANSACTION_INTENT_CONSUMED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); intent resolution (absent or not-admitted intent) → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-60] [A-74.1]; signer binding (session agent = intent `actorId`) → `RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`; inherited rc.1 preconditions (adapter identity §8.7, …) → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–3 (always admitted at `maxEvents`) |
| 15 | `TRANSACTION_OUTCOME_RECORDED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); intent resolution → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-60] [A-74.1]; signer binding (session agent = intent `actorId`, or `attesterId` when it names an admitted agent) → `RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`; inherited rc.1 preconditions → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; ≤ 16 outcomes per intent → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–3 |
| 16 | `RECEIPT_RECORDED` ◇ | schema also requires inherited `runtimeSessionId`/`controlEpoch` = nested (`EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-61.3]); T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); intent resolution → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-60] [A-74.1]; signer binding (session agent = `issuerAgentId`; the inherited-session clause is satisfied by construction [A-74.3]) → `RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`; inherited rc.1 preconditions → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; ≤ 16 receipts per intent → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–3 |
| 17 | `OBLIGATION_CREATED` ▲ | outer session fence → `SESSION_FENCED` (first of the type's checks, ahead of every precondition [A-82]; T1 and T3 precede it as envelope checks [A-172.1]); administrative authorization (§8.4) and rc.1 preconditions, including "no obligation already for `sourceIntentId`" [A-88.1] → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (first for a source intent spends its reservation, `ΔS = 0`; a repeat is `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-88.1]) |

| 21 | `OUTCOME_OBSERVATION_RECORDED` ▲ | outer session fence (first of the type's checks; T1 and T3 precede it [A-172.1]); administrative authorization and rc.1 preconditions → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; ≤ 64 observations per intent → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–3 |
| 22 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_CREATED` ▲ | outer session fence (first of the type's checks; T1 and T3 precede it [A-172.1]); administrative authorization and rc.1 preconditions, including "no duty yet for the source intent" [A-88.1] → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (first for a source intent spends its reservation, `ΔS = 0`; a repeat is `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-88.1]) [A-171.1] |
| 23 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_ASSIGNED` ▲ | outer session fence (first of the type's checks; T1 and T3 precede it [A-172.1]); administrative authorization and rc.1 preconditions → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; ≤ 64 assignments per duty → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (first for a source intent spends its reservation, `ΔS = 0`; a repeat, a second assignment of the one duty, is growth, `ΔS = +1`, and refused `CAPACITY_CONTROL_ONLY` at `maxEvents` [A-88.2]) |
| 24 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_REVIEW_CLOSED` ▲ | outer session fence (first of the type's checks; T1 and T3 precede it [A-172.1]); administrative authorization and rc.1 preconditions → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; ≤ 64 reviews per duty → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–3 (always admitted at `maxEvents`) |

| 29 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_POLICY_ACTIVATED` ▲ | §13.1 list (schema including the descriptor's literal fields → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID`; the re-derived descriptor equality and every other step → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-129.2]; outer session fence `SESSION_FENCED` first of the type's checks, ahead of every item of the §13.1 list [A-82] [A-202.1], T1 and T3 preceding it as envelope checks [A-172.1]); capacity steps 1–4 (growth) |
| 30, 31 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_DISPOSITION_RECORDED`, `ATTEMPT_DUTY_CONTEST_RECORDED` ▲ | §13.2 list (schema including the `version` and `rulesHash` literals → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID`; the other steps `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-129.1]; attester session keeps §13.2 codes; outer session fence `SESSION_FENCED` first of the type's checks, ahead of every item of the §13.2 list (the attester session keeps §13.2's codes) [A-82] [A-202.1], T1 and T3 preceding it as envelope checks [A-172.1]); capacity steps 1–3 (within the duty quota) |
| 32 | `BUDGET_DECLARED` ◆ | schema per §9.1 (`EVENT_DATA_INVALID`); step 0 (`PR_eff` always, §6.5.3); control authorization (quorum per §6.5.3); T2 [A-125.1]; duplicate `budgetId`, 257th budget → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity step 1 |
| 33, 34 | `BUDGET_UPDATED`, `BUDGET_CLOSED` ◆ | schema (`EVENT_DATA_INVALID`, incl. post-update cap rule); step 0 (`PR_eff` of §6.5.3 for 33; never for 34); control authorization (quorum per §6.5.3); T2 [A-125.1]; budget not ACTIVE → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity step 1 |
| 35 | `STATE_CHECKPOINT` ◆ | step 0 (`isCheckpoint`); control authorization (quorum per §6.5.3: the full `M_eff(STATE_CHECKPOINT)`, default 1, `LAPSED` keys counting [A-174.1] [A-107.2]); T2 [A-125.1]; `head` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; unique `checkpointId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `stateRoot`/`recordCount` → `STATE_ROOT_MISMATCH`; `projectionHead` → `PROJECTION_HEAD_MISMATCH`; capacity step 1 |
| 36, 37 | `COUNTERPARTY_REGISTERED`, `COUNTERPARTY_RETIRED` ◆ | schema (keys; §3.3 declaration test → `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-32.2]); step 0 (`PR_eff` of §6.5.3 for 37); control authorization (quorum per §6.5.3); T2 [A-125.1]; ACTIVE re-registration / unknown or RETIRED id → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; for 36, committed, owner or attester credential under a policy (§6.5.7: while `P_w(p)` is present, a credential in `creds(K(p))`, whose digest is in `D(p)`, or in `attCreds(P(p))`) → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-110.2] [A-117.4] [A-125.1]; capacity step 1 |
| 38 | `REQUEST_REFUSED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`REFUSAL_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); actor binding → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `evaluationTime == timestamp` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `expectedHistoryHead` = head before → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; CORE/BUDGET re-derivation → `REFUSAL_NOT_REPRODUCED`; CONNECTOR `code == "CONNECTOR_REFUSED"` (schema); unique `refusalId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (growth) |
| 39 | `EFFECT_RECORDED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`EFFECT_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); measurer binding → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; COUNTERPARTY envelope binding (non-null `detailDigest` = an earlier accepted type-40 `envelopeDigest`, same `intentId` and counterparty; `amount` equal) → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-36.1]; SESSION actor binding (signing session's agent = intent `actorId`) → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-37.1]; intent admitted → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `observedAt ≤ timestamp` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; ≤ 64 effects per intent → `EFFECT_LIMIT`; unique `effectId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (growth) |
| 40 | `COUNTERPARTY_RECEIPT_RECORDED` ◇ | T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); counterparty ACTIVE with `keyId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; envelope signature → `COUNTERPARTY_SIGNATURE_INVALID`; S(`RECORD_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); `historyId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `logicalId`, intent admitted, `intentDigest` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `envelope.observedAt ≤ timestamp` → `TRANSITION_INVALID` [A-45.1]; `previousReceiptDigest` chain → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (growth) |
| 42 | `RECONCILIATION_OBSERVED` ◇ | schema also refuses a missing or ill-formed `observationAuthorization`, `hash` or `signature` (`EVENT_DATA_INVALID` [A-89.3]); T1 and T3 precede this list (A.2 [A-172.1]); S(`OBSERVATION_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID`); reconciler binding (`reconcilerAgentId` = session agent) → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; `expectedHistoryHead` = head before → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; MATCHED: `position < p`, type 13 or 38, its §12.2 actor = `call.actorId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; any accepted observation with the same `(streamId, eventRef)` (whole history, across reconcilers) and an equal digest → `OBSERVATION_DUPLICATE` (otherwise accepted; `equivocates` = the earliest such observation's position when one exists, else `null`) [A-86.1] [A-89.5]; unique `observationId` → `TRANSITION_INVALID`; capacity steps 1–4 (growth) [A-71.3] |

**A.5 Rejection position of an incomplete migrated prefix.** [A-130.1] A history that ends before `migratedFrom.position` is rejected when its replay completes with `TRANSITION_INVALID` (reason `migrated prefix incomplete`) at position `eventCount`, the first missing position (§16.2 step 3); the store's `INVALID_HISTORY {position}` on open names the same position. The last present event was accepted and applied; naming it would call an accepted event rejected.

## Appendix B — Closed `data` schemas for the 42 types

Notation: `CA` = `controlAuthorization: {keyId: Identifier, signature: Signature}` (§6.1) **plus optional `ACA`** = `additionalControlAuthorizations?: [{keyId: Identifier, signature: Signature}]{1..63}` (§6.5.3; its shape is C4, its presence — iff `M_eff(e) ≥ 2` — a replay rule under §6.5.3, not a schema rule; excluded from the §6.1 effect; `EVENT_DATA_INVALID` at C4 inside a migrated prefix, coded against `migratedFrom`) [A-95.3] [A-92.0] [A-91.5]; `AA` = `administrativeAuthorization` (`irontrellis-administrative-authorization/2`, fields per HF1 §8.4); `RA` = `recordAuthorization: {runtimeSessionId, credentialKeyId, controlEpoch: U53, hash: ContentHash, signature}` (§8.8); `SB` = the session binding `runtimeSessionId, credentialKeyId, controlEpoch`. Every Amount is a §3.3 string. "HF1 §6 fields" means the rc.1-final closed field list of that type in the HF1 §6 type table (with HF1 §6.1 genesis, §6.2 grants, §6.3 evidence references, §6.4 obligation record; every key listed there is required unless marked `?`), unchanged except as stated (incorporated by reference; E-1 closed for HF1). Prefix rules of §16.2 apply only where `inPrefix(p)`.

| # | type | `data` |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | `DEPLOYMENT_INITIALIZED` | `{domain: {protocol: "irontrellis", version: "2", deploymentId}, adapterPolicyHash, canonicalLineageId, versions: <§5.1 literal set>, policyVersion, rootRecognitionPolicy: "declared-principal-root/0.2", globalPolicySourceId, timeSource: "EVENT_TIMESTAMP", finality: "LOCAL_ONLY", ownerKeys: [{keyId, scheme, credential}]{1..64}, nextKeyDigests?: [ContentHash], capacity?: {maxEvents: U53\|null, maxStateRecords?: U53\|null}, migratedFrom?: {format, historyHash, position, recordHash, genesis, genesisEventId, genesisTimestamp, versions, sourceAttestation: <the source pin's `irontrellis-head-attestation/1`, HF1 §9> \| null, migrationId: ContentHash}, checkpointPolicy?: {everyEvents: U53 ≥ 1 \| null}, projection?: "irontrellis-projection/2" \| null, ownerPolicy?: <the §6.5.2 schema: threshold, byType?, keyClasses?, labels?, acknowledged?, recovery?, humanFloor?, attesters?, attestations?: [HumanAttestation]{1..64}, each with `historyId?` absent at position 0> (forbidden with `migratedFrom`, §6.5.2 rule 9) [A-95.3] [A-105.2] [A-115.2], sessionTimeHorizonMs?: U53 ≥ 1 \| null [A-179.1 (A-22.6)]}` (`sourceAttestation` and `migrationId` per §16.2 step 4 [A-40.2–3, as drafted]) |
| 1 | `PRINCIPAL_CREATED` | `{principalId, CA}` |
| 2 | `AGENT_CREATED` | `{agentId, principalId, controllerId, initialControlEpoch: U53, CA}` |
| 3 | `ROLE_CREATED` | `{roleId, principalId, exclusive: true, CA}` |
| 4 | `SUCCESSION_RULE_DECLARED` | HF1 §6 fields + `CA` (the §6.1 effect is stored as `succession.rule` [A-170.8]) |
| 5 | `AGENT_APPOINTED` | HF1 §6 fields + `CA` |
| 6 | `AGENT_UNAPPOINTED` | HF1 §6 fields + `CA` |
| 7 | `ROLE_TRANSFERRED` | HF1 §6 fields + `CA` |
| 8 | `RUNTIME_SESSION_ADMITTED` | HF1 §6 fields (`sessionId, agentId, controllerId, controlEpoch, credentialKeyId, …`) + `credentialScheme: "ed25519" \| "eip191-personal-sign-keccak256"` + `credentialPublicKey: PublicKey32` (ed25519) or `credentialAddress: EthereumAddress` (eip191) + `expiresAt: U53` (required outside a migrated prefix; inside it absent or a U53 copied unchanged; never `null` [A-62.1] [A-73]) + `CA` |
| 9 | `CONTROL_EPOCH_ADVANCED` | HF1 §6 fields + `CA` |
| 10 | `AUTHORITY_GRANTED` | `{grant: <HF1 §6.2 grant; constraints.maxAmount / maxCumulativeAmount as Amount strings>, CA}` |
| 11 | `AUTHORITY_REVOKED` | HF1 §6 fields + `CA` |
| 12 | `TRANSACTION_INTENT_DECLARED` | HF1 fields (`intentId, nonce, actorId, action, resource, roleId, roleTenureId, adapterProfile, amount?: Amount, counterpartyId?, termsCommitment?`) + `metric?: Identifier` + `declarationAuthorization: {SB, hash: ContentHash, signature}` (`null` iff in a migrated prefix) |
| 13 | `TRANSACTION_INTENT_ADMITTED` | HF1 fields (`intentId, runtimeAuthorizationHash, runtimeSignature, expectedHistoryHead: Head, authorizationProof: <§8.3 proof, Amount strings>, …`) |
| 14 | `TRANSACTION_INTENT_CONSUMED` | HF1 fields (adapter identity §8.7) + `RA` (absent iff in a migrated prefix) |

| 16 | `RECEIPT_RECORDED` | HF1 fields (`intentId, issuerAgentId, runtimeSessionId, controlEpoch, receiptContentHash, receiptArtifactCommitment, …`; receipt schema `irontrellis-receipt/2`) + `RA` (absent iff in a migrated prefix; inherited `runtimeSessionId`/`controlEpoch` equal the nested values) |
| 17 | `OBLIGATION_CREATED` | HF1 fields (`actorId, record: {obligationId, …}`) + `AA` |
| 18 | `OBLIGATION_PERFORMANCE_ASSIGNED` | HF1 fields (`obligationId, toAgentId, …`) + `AA` |
| 19 | `OBLIGATION_STATUS_RECORDED` | HF1 fields (`actorId, obligationId, action, …`) + `AA` (`request.action = data.action`) |

| 21 | `OUTCOME_OBSERVATION_RECORDED` | HF1 fields (`intentId, …`; E5/E6 profiles) + `AA` |
| 22 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_CREATED` | HF1 fields (`actorId, record: {dutyId, …}`) + `AA` |
| 23 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_ASSIGNED` | HF1 fields (`dutyId, toAgentId, …`) + `AA` |
| 24 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_REVIEW_CLOSED` | HF1 fields (`actorId, dutyId, …`) + `AA` |
| 25 | `ANCHOR_RECORDED` | HF1 §6 fields (`anchoredHead` (head), `anchor`, `receipt`, `anchoredAt`; HF1 §10) + `CA` |
| 26 | `OWNER_KEYS_ROTATED` | HF1 §6 fields (`ownerKeys` (1..64, each `ed25519` key per the §3.3 declaration test [A-32.2]), `reason?`, `nextKeyDigests?`, `nextKeyAuthorizations?`; HF1 §7.1–7.2 as read in §6.5.4; digest version `irontrellis-owner-key-digest/1`) + `ownerPolicy?: <the §6.5.2 schema, as row 0; `historyId?` required on every statement not carried from the pre-event policy>` [A-95.3] [A-105.2] [A-115.2] + `CA` (`nextKeyAuthorizations` and `additionalControlAuthorizations` excluded from `effectHash`, §6.1 [A-95.3]) |
| 27 | `WITNESSES_BOUND` | HF1 §6 / §10.1 fields (`witnesses: [{url, verifierKey, name?, allowInsecureHttp?, registration?}]{1..16}, policy: {quorum, requireOnOpen, maxUnwitnessedEvents}`) + `CA` |
| 28 | `WITNESSES_UNBOUND` | HF1 §6 / §10.1 fields (`verifierKeys`{1..16}, `policy`, `acceptedLoss?: {reason, heads: [{verifierKey, head}]{1..16}}`) + `CA` |
| 29 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_POLICY_ACTIVATED` | `{actorId, descriptor: <§13.1>, descriptorHash, activationAuthorityId, AA}` |
| 30 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_DISPOSITION_RECORDED` | `{version: "continuity-attempt-disposition/1", rulesHash, dutyId, actorId, dispositionAuthorityId, finding: {challenge: <§13.2, purpose DISPOSITION>, runtimeSignature}, AA}` |
| 31 | `ATTEMPT_DUTY_CONTEST_RECORDED` | same as 30 with `purpose: CONTEST` and the contest `operation` |
| 32 | `BUDGET_DECLARED` | `{budgetId, subjects: Subjects, metric: Identifier \| null, period, amountCap: Amount\|null, countCap: U53\|null, perOperationCap: Amount\|null, notBefore: U53\|null, expiresAt: U53\|null, CA}` (§9.1) |
| 33 | `BUDGET_UPDATED` | `{budgetId, subjects, amountCap, countCap, perOperationCap, expiresAt, CA}` |
| 34 | `BUDGET_CLOSED` | `{budgetId, CA}` |
| 35 | `STATE_CHECKPOINT` | `{checkpointId, head: {hash, position}, stateRoot, recordCount: U53, derivationVersion: "irontrellis-state/2", projectionHead: ContentHash\|null, CA}` |
| 36 | `COUNTERPARTY_REGISTERED` | `{counterpartyId, keys: [{keyId, scheme: "ed25519", credential: PublicKey32}]{1..16}, CA}` |
| 37 | `COUNTERPARTY_RETIRED` | `{counterpartyId, CA}` |
| 38 | `REQUEST_REFUSED` | `{refusalId, refusalSource, request: {actorId, action, resource, claimedAt, amount?, counterpartyId?, termsCommitment?, metric?}, evaluationTime: U53, expectedHistoryHead: Head, decision, code, budgetId?, connector?: {connectorId, refusalCode, detailDigest}, SB, refusalAuthorization: {hash, signature}}` (§10.1) |
| 39 | `EFFECT_RECORDED` | `{effectId, intentId, measurer: {kind, id}, metric, unit, amount: Amount, observedAt: U53, detailDigest: ContentHash\|null, SB, effectAuthorization: {hash, signature}}` (§10.2) |
| 40 | `COUNTERPARTY_RECEIPT_RECORDED` | `{intentId, envelope: <§11.2 envelope>, SB, recordAuthorization: {hash, signature}}` (§11.2; this wrapper's `recordAuthorization` has no nested binding, unlike `RA` of 14–16) |
| 42 | `RECONCILIATION_OBSERVED` | `{observationId, reconcilerAgentId, source: {streamId, eventRef, eventDigest: ContentHash}, call: {actorId, action?, resource?, observedAt?: U53, amount?: Amount, unsupplied: [Identifier]}, match: {result: "MATCHED" \| "UNMATCHED", position?: U53}, expectedHistoryHead: Head, SB, observationAuthorization: {hash, signature}}` (§10.3; `action`/`resource`/`observedAt` absent iff named in `unsupplied`; `position` iff MATCHED) [A-71.1] |

Every row that carries `CA` (rows 1–11, 20, 25–28, 32–37) may carry `ACA` under the notation above; no other row may [A-95.3] [A-92.0]. Control events 1–11, 20, 25–28, 32–37 in a migrated prefix of a **format-1** source additionally carry `sourceControlAuthorization` (the source's `/1` authorization, verbatim); it is forbidden elsewhere. The 11-type lifecycle reservation set of §5.2.1 is rc.1-final `capacity.ts`'s verbatim, copied by the builder into DEVIATIONS; it contains types 23, 17 and 22 [A-72.2] [A-171.1].

## Appendix C — Challenge and signed/hashed objects with their domain strings

| object | version / domain string | signer or use | definition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Control challenge | `irontrellis-control-authorization/2` | owner key, `hashCanonical`; every co-signer of `additionalControlAuthorizations` signs the same challenge (§6.5.3) [A-95.4] | §6.1: `{version, domain, eventId, eventType, timestamp, previousHead, effectHash}` |
| Runtime authorization challenge | `irontrellis-runtime-authorization/2` | session (admission) | §8.2 |
| Authorization proof | `irontrellis-authorization-proof/2` | byte-equality on replay | §8.3 |
| Administrative authorization | `irontrellis-administrative-authorization/2` | session (▲ events); `transitionEffectHash` per §8.4 | §8.4, HF1 §8.4 |
| Receipt payload / artifact commitment | `irontrellis-receipt/2`; `irontrellis-receipt-artifact-commitment/2` | issuer | §8.5 |
| Intent declaration challenge | `irontrellis-intent-declaration/2` | session (type 12) | §8.6 (with `eventId, eventType, timestamp` [A-54]) |
| Lifecycle authorization challenge | `irontrellis-lifecycle-authorization/2` | session (types 14–16) | §8.8 |
| Adapter identity | `continuity-adapter-idempotency/0.2`, `continuity-adapter-submission/0.2`, `continuity-adapter-no-effect/0.2` | derivation | §8.7 |
| Refusal challenge | `irontrellis-refusal-authorization/2` | session (type 38) | §10.1 (with `eventId, eventType, timestamp` [A-54]) |
| Reconciliation challenge | `irontrellis-reconciliation-authorization/2` | session (type 42) | §10.3 (A-54 form) [A-71.2] |
| Effect challenge | `irontrellis-effect-authorization/2` | session (type 39) | §10.2 (with `eventId, eventType, timestamp` [A-54]) |
| Counterparty envelope | `irontrellis-counterparty-receipt/2` | counterparty key over `envelopeSigningHash` | §11.2 |
| Counterparty wrapper challenge | `irontrellis-counterparty-record-authorization/2` | session (type 40) | §11.2 (with `eventId, eventType, timestamp` [A-54]) |
| Head attestation body | `irontrellis-head-attestation/2` | owner key (pin, anchors) | §7.4 |
| Witness head statement | `irontrellis-head-statement/1` (protocol `irontrellis-witness/1`) | owner key | §7.4 [A-5] (the witness's epoch records carry `policy?` and `policyKnown`; its `verifyRotationEvent` result `policyRotation`, `restoration`, `recovery`, `stateChecked: false` [A-100.1] [A-111.3] [A-120.1]) |
| Owner key digest | `irontrellis-owner-key-digest/1` | rotation pre-commitment | §5 row 26, HF1 §7.2 |
| Human attestation body | `irontrellis-human-attestation/1` | attester key (`attesters` entry of the same `ownerPolicy`) over `hashCanonical(body)`, body = the attestation without `signature`; `historyId?` is in the body (absent at position 0; `recordHash(0)` on every statement set after the genesis that is not carried) | §6.5.2 [A-93.1] [A-95.4] [A-115.2] |
| Record chain | `irontrellis-record/2` | hash prefix | §4.3 |
| Projection chain | `irontrellis-projection/2` | hash prefix (217-byte preimage) | §12.3 |
| State derivation / snapshot / export | `irontrellis-state/2`, `irontrellis-snapshot/2`, `irontrellis-export/2` | labels | §7.3, §7.5, §15 |
| Migration plan / fence | `irontrellis-migration-plan/1`, `irontrellis-migrated/1` | side files (the plan also carries `migrationId` [A-40.3]) | §16.2 |
| Source head attestation (migrated genesis) | `irontrellis-head-attestation/1` (HF1 §9; object as written, `domain` presence per HF1 §9 [A-80]) | source owner key, verified on replay under `migratedFrom.genesis.ownerKeys` | §16.2 step 4 [A-40.2] |
| Migration id | none (32 random bytes, ContentHash-shaped) | migrator, at plan time | §16.2 steps 1, 4 [A-40.3] |
| Generated ids (API) | `hashCanonical([<kind literal>, actorId, operationId])`; literals `irontrellis-intent-id/2`, `irontrellis-effect-id/2`, `irontrellis-refusal-id/2` [A-69] [A-77]; `hashCanonical(["irontrellis-observation-id/2", reconcilerAgentId, streamId, eventRef, eventDigest])` [A-71.6] | API derivation of `intentId`, `effectId`, `refusalId`, `observationId` | §10.2, §10.3 [A-37.2] |
| Rendered-request digest | `connector.detailDigest` = `sha256` of the canonical rendered request | hook/gateway (CONNECTOR refusal) | §10.1.1 item 10 [A-42.2] |
| Duty descriptor | `continuity-attempt-disposition/1` (criteriaProfile `local-investigation/1`) | byte-equality | §13.1 |
| Duty finding challenge | `continuity-duty-finding/1` | attester session | §13.2 |
| Duty evidence index | `continuity-duty-evidence-index/1` | hashed | §13.2 |
| Duty view | `continuity-attempt-duty-view/1` | view | §13.3 |
| Query envelope | `continuity-query-envelope/0.2` | queries | §5.1 |
| Canonical text | `continuity-canonical/0.2` | all hashing | §3.1 |
| Rules record hash | `rulesHash = 0xf7fb…5f94` (keccak-256 literal) | constant | §13.0 |

The anchor statement `irontrellis-anchor-statement/1`, the anchor verify report `irontrellis-anchor-verify-report/1`, the profile manifest and the ZK ABI objects are not Core objects and are specified in the companion documents named in §7.4; the evidence digest `evidenceDigest = sha256(bundle)` of a human attestation is a commitment to an opaque octet string, not a canonical-text hash (§6.5.2 [A-102 X-13]) [A-186.4].

| superseded passage (SPEC r2.3 unless stated) | replaced by |

See E-15 for a residual question on the verification keys.

Whether `ATTEMPT_DUTY_ASSIGNED` and `OBLIGATION_CREATED` change `S(p)` follows from that set, not from this text. *Closed in r4 (A-72, replacing the r3 closure under A-64)* and *re-closed in r5 (A-171.2):* A-72 with A-88: `ΔS = 1 + ΔlifecycleReserved + ΔdutyReserved`; the first 17/23 (and 22 per A-171.1) spends the reservation; a repeat 17 or 22 is `TRANSITION_INVALID` before capacity; a repeat 23 is growth. A-64.1 second sentence and A-64.2 withdrawn.

- **E-10 Witness protocol text.** *Open in r3 (A-70.3); no build dependency before G2.5.* A-5 reads `irontrellis-head-statement/1` through WITNESS §9.1/§9.2/§9.9; §7.4 carries A-5's reading verbatim. rc.1-final `docs/WITNESS.md` (with its §9 "Protocol reference") is in the rc.1-final source tree beside HF1 but was not checked in this pass; the gap stays open. *r5 (A-177):* format 2's only obligations on the witness text are A-5, A-100.1, A-111.3 and A-120.1.
- **E-11 Packaging gap (X-13).** `reference-0.3`, `readability.py`, the legacy fixture and `generate/lib.mjs` are to be resent by the coordinator; no spec action.
- **E-12 A-47.3 glossary wording** (§3.2). *Closed in r3 (A-70.1): reading (i), two hash values.* The drafted line equates `historyId` and `genesisHash` (both `recordHash(0)`) yet calls "the three" distinct values. Reading (i): "the three" are the three names, which denote two values; reading (ii): three distinct values, which contradicts the line's own equation and §13.1. No hash definition changes under either reading. Editorial ruling needed.
- **E-13 Original attempt time of a drained refusal** (§10.1.1 item 9 (b)). *Closed in r3 (A-68): reading (ii), for every drain.* The draft keeps it in `request.claimedAt`; G2 modification (i) sends it to the side log, "never into `request.claimedAt` (which is part of the re-derived request)", for a drain by a process that does not hold the actor's session. Reading (i): a drain by a process that holds the actor's session keeps the draft rule; reading (ii): G2's reason applies to every drain, and `request.claimedAt` never carries the original time. Architect ruling needed; the outcome affects the re-derived request and therefore `REFUSAL_NOT_REPRODUCED`/`NOT_REPRODUCED_AT_DRAIN`.
- **E-14 `<kind literal>` of derived ids** (§10.2, A-37.2). *Closed in r3 (A-69): `irontrellis-{intent,effect,refusal}-id/2`; r4 adds `irontrellis-observation-id/2` (A-71.6, A-77).* The draft gives the form `hashCanonical([<kind literal>, actorId, operationId])` but not the literals for `intentId`, `effectId` and `refusalId`. Reading (i): each implementation chooses its constants; reading (ii): common literals still to be specified, so that the TypeScript engine and the Python port derive the same ids. API rule only; replay is unaffected.
- **E-15 Owner keys for verifying a format-1 prefix** (§16.2 step 4, A-40.1–2). *Closed in r3 (A-63): reading (i); rotated or witness-bound format-1 sources are refused at plan time (`MIGRATION_UNSUPPORTED {reason: SOURCE_KEY_ROTATED | SOURCE_WITNESS_BOUND}`); lifting it is a later addendum.* Both points verify under `migratedFrom.genesis.ownerKeys` on the premise "no rotation can occur in the prefix (§16.2 last paragraph / HF1 §11)". HF1 places that restriction on a migrated **legacy** prefix (HF1 §7.1 item 4, §7.2, §10.1, §11), while a native format-1 history may contain `OWNER_KEYS_ROTATED` (including commitment-only rotations) and `WITNESSES_BOUND`. Reading (i): such a format-1 source cannot be migrated (§16.2's "no rotation or witness binding inside the prefix"), and genesis-key verification is complete; reading (ii): such sources can be migrated, and their post-rotation control authorizations and pin attestation do not verify under the genesis keys. Architect ruling needed before the builder pins the format-1 → 2 twin at G2.5.
- **E-16 Export carriers `from.fileBytes` and `attestations.toCheckpoint`** (A-75.3). *Open, post-rc.2 additive.* The §15 shape carries no `B(from)` and no checkpoint after `to`; a verifier from `from > 0` reports the byte tests as not judged and `to` is authenticated through `attestations.to`. Both carriers are nested in closed records: a format-2 addendum under §19's unreleased frame, or a format-3 item after release.
- **E-17 A-71 conformance.** *Open in r4 until POST-E5.* Type 42 is specified (§10.3) but its vector group, pinned root and the Python checker's support land only after E5 closes; until then it is not conformance-tested. *Closed in r5 (A-171.3):* A-71 vectors and the `accepts-observation-history` root landed after E5 (G6.1 ordering: done, verified; g25 pins +3/+2 records per A-87). The group gained `rejects-duplicate-after-equivocation` and `accepts-second-equivocation`, and the checker default is `obs_dup=any` (the `earliest` reading is withdrawn; it may stay in the readings matrix as a diagnostic) [A-86.4].
- **E-18 Corroboration by several reconcilers.** *Open; post-rc.2.* Corroboration of one provider event by several reconcilers (today: a second reconciler's identical report is `OBSERVATION_DUPLICATE` under A-89.5, and a different digest an equivocation). A format-3 item or a nested additive field. [A-89.5]

- **E-19 Dedicated human-attestation lifecycle type and status records (placement C).** *Open; post-rc.2; not adopted.* Re-worded by A-105.4: "dedicated human-attestation lifecycle event type and status records (placement C); not needed for renewal, revocation or reclassification, which are policy rotations (A-96); kept for a deployment whose status churn makes a rotation per change impractical; post-rc.2." §6.5.4 states that E-19 is not adopted for renewals, revocations, reclassifications, attester changes, floor changes or `recovery` changes, which are all policy rotations with an unchanged key set [A-96.0]; A-93.6's dependency on HF1 §7.2 is met by the policy rotation (Appendix D). [A-95.8] [A-105.4]
- **E-20 Digest-reference record for the external install-acceptance packet and the anchor statement.** *Open; post-rc.2, additive, nested.* An optional record carrying a digest reference to the e-signature acceptance packet (`irontrellis-install-acceptance/1`, outside Core) or an anchor statement; type 42 is never a carrier for it. Unchanged by G6.6 (A-145.3). [A-95.8] [A-95 table]
- **E-21 The PHL ZK relation over `(historyId, epoch record, attestation body)`.** *Open; outside Core.* A relation a prover could state over the attestation evidence Core commits (§6.5.5); no Core text depends on it. The ZK ABI companion document (P.9) is where such a relation would be specified. [A-95.8] [A-105.4] [AR-09]
- **E-22 An `eip191-personal-sign-keccak256` attestation subject scheme (X-12).** *Open; post-rc.2.* rc.2 attests Ed25519 subjects only; §6.5.2 and §6.5.5 state the limitation (`verify` lists owner keys of a scheme the attestation profile cannot name). [A-105.4] [A-102 X-12]
- **E-23 Trusted-time evidence for attestation intervals (invariant 26).** *Open; outside Core.* Core reads no clock: an attestation interval is judged against the event's signed timestamp, owner-side time is a stated limit (§6.5.6; Appendix F items 26 and 37; Appendix G G.6 "Owner-side time"), and the `verify --as-of` and time listings of §18 are the Core-side mitigation. Anchor-time bracketing (A-181, §18) is chain evidence about publication, never a clock for replay. [A-105.4] [A-109.3]
- **E-24 Quorum recommitment.** *Open; post-rc.2.* An opt-in under which a policy rotation signed by `≥ max(2, M_rot)` `HUMAN` keys may replace the commitment of the epoch in force (it would close Appendix F item 38 at the cost of HF1's one-key guarantee). Until adopted, out of a committed epoch the key set changes only by a rotation that reveals a committed key with proof of possession (§6.5.4; Appendix G G.5 "Recovery and commitments"). [A-115.5] [A-110.4]

The item gains the type-35 `MAX_UNWITNESSED` interaction (A-119.2): A-24.1 is amended so that `MAX_UNWITNESSED` never refuses a `STATE_CHECKPOINT` (§6.1 [A-176.2]); A-22.3 is not amended (a checkpoint is still `CLOCK_INVALID` while the head is ahead of the writer's clock, which A-179's `ownerTime` relies on); DEV-94 closes in build2.
